provides recommendations for new directions to explore.- Demonstrates good understanding of and conducts research on industry trends and innovations in graphics to ensure solutions and deliverables align with best practices.- Writes clear and detailed technical documentation and feature descriptions for complex projects to guide users and/or customers to use or implement software. - Consults with internal or external users and third-party vendors to guide implementation and ensure alignment with their needs and goals.# Required Skills and Experience- A strong undergraduate or postgraduate degree, ideally in Computer Science or Electronic Engineering, but other academic backgrounds will be considered with meaningful experience.- GPU architecture.- GPU APIs knowledge (Vulkan/Direct3D/OpenGL/Direct X / OpenCL etc.)- Strong programming in C/C++.- Proficiency in a high-level shell or scripting language such as Python.- Flexibility and a willingness to work with new tools.- Ability to communicate clearly with a wide variety of audiences.- Proficiency in problem solving and debugging skills.- Practical, organized and analytical approach to work.# Preferred Skills and Experience- Games/graphics application development.- Graphics driver development or modelling experience. - Post-silicon enablement and bring-up.- Prior experience in working in emulation environments for development and debug.- Knowledge of real-time computing graphics technology- Comfortable to work close to the hardware- Device driver developing experience- Working experience on large scale system software 公司名称是飞腾公司， 公司规模为1000-9999人， 属于半导体/芯片行业。(qi)

AI 算法原型的工程化实现：将现有 Python 原型算法转换为高性能 C++ 实现，分析 Python 代码逻辑，设计 C++ 架构方案，实现性能和稳定性上超越原 Python 实现；4. 参与跨部门合作，确保业务需求得到满足，确保代码质量与项目进度；5. 编写技术文档，包括部署指南、性能评估报告等；岗位要求：1. 计算机等相关专业硕士学历及以上学位，2年以上工作经验，熟悉C++、Python语言，有开发经验，有良好的编程习惯和代码组织能力；2. 有AI模型部署经验，了解常用的图像算法、深度学习CNN、Transformer等，至少熟悉一种深度学习框架，如tf, pytorch，至少掌握一种深度学习推理框架如onnx, tensorrt；3. 技术基础扎实，对数据结构、算法设计有基本了解；具备代码优化相关经验；4. 持续分析代码工程中的瓶颈(如内存泄露、冗余计算、低效IO等)，通过算法改进、数据结构优化、并行化等提升系统整体效率；5. 具有cuda代码读写能力者优先，能利用GPU优化加速算法，了解cuda代码优化技巧优先；6. 有热情，积极主动，自驱力强，有较强的责任心和良好的执行能力，具备优秀的沟通协调能力及团队合作精神。 公司名称是上海帮图信息科技， 公司规模为100-499人， 属于计算机软件行业。(qi)
